\displaystyle=\sigma^{2}{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\frac{1}{n}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bigg)=\sum_{k=1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}.

Therefore, it suffices to show that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{k=1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}-\sum_{k>r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}\bigg|\lesssim\frac{r\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}.

We will demonstrate this result assuming
\lambda_{1}
through
\lambda_{r}
are all positive; this is with no significant loss of generality as the argument is straightforward to adapt and significantly more cumbersome if this is not the case.

By the Poincare Separation Theorem (Corollary 4.3.37 of
Horn and Johnson 2012
), it holds that

\displaystyle\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}\leq\lambda_{k}^{\perp}\leq\widehat{\lambda}_{k}.

Therefore
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}\geq\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}
for
k\geq r+1
, where we have used the fact that
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
is positive. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}\leq\sum_{k=1}^{r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}+\sum_{k=r+1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}+\sum_{k=r+1}^{n}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}.

Similarly,

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}

\displaystyle\geq\sum_{k=1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}}=\sum_{k=r+1}^{n}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{k=1}^{n-r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}-\sum_{k=r+1}^{n}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k=1}^{r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}.

The result follows by noting that
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}\gtrsim\lambda_{j}
by
1
.
∎

B.3
Proof of Lemma
8.3

Proof.

We will bound each quantity in turn.

•

Bounding
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{1}
.
Write

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}+\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a},

where we have implicitly used the fact that
\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}=0
.
By
Theorem

8.2
and
4
it holds that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}

\displaystyle=\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}+\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}.

Plugging this in yields

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg)\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=:\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}.

In what follows we bound each of the quantities
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}
and
\alpha_{3}
in turn.

–

The term
\alpha_{1}
.
By viewing
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
as a fixed parameter
\lambda
, the quantity
\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}
has expectation
\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\lambda)
. The following lemma demonstrates that this concentration occurs with high probability when
\lambda
is replaced with
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
.

Lemma B.2
.

Instate the conditions of
Lemma

8.2
. With probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}.

Proof.

See
Section

D.1
.
∎

As a result,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg)\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}},

(19)

where we have applied Cauchy-Schwarz.

–

The term
\alpha_{2}
. Note that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{2}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k\neq j}\|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}\|\big\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\big\|\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\leq\max_{k\neq j}\big\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\big\|\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}.

Observe that by rotational invariance,
(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}
,
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}
and
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}
are all independent. Therefore, again viewing
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
as a fixed parameter, the term above is a mean-zero product of functions of Gaussian random matrices. The following lemma shows that this term concentrates at an appropriate rate.

Lemma B.3
.

Instate the conditions of
Lemma

8.2
. With probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}.

Proof.

See
Section

D.2
.
∎

Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

(20)

where the final line follows from Cauchy-Schwarz.

–

The term
\alpha_{3}
.
By Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j}\big\|\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|.

Observe that by
Lemma

8.1
we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=2-2\langle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j},\bm{u}_{j}\rangle^{2}=2\big(1-(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}\big)\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}.

(21)

Next, the term
\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}
is a sum of mean-zero independent random matrices. The following lemma bounds this term by appealing to a standard concentration argument.

Lemma B.4
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

3.1
. Then with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma r\sqrt{\log(n)}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

Proof.

See
Section

D.3
.
∎

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\sigma r\sqrt{\log(n)}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}.

(22)

Combining (
19
) , (
20
) and (
22
) results in the bound

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{1}|\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}},

(23)

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.

•

Bounding
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{2}
. We decompose via

\displaystyle\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}

\displaystyle=:\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}.

We bound each in turn.

–

The term
\beta_{1}
. By a similar argument as in (
21
), with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
, we have that

\displaystyle|\beta_{1}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j}\|\frac{\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\frac{\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}\frac{\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}},

(24)

where the final bound holds from standard Gaussian concentration inequalities and the fact that
\frac{1}{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}
is equal in distribution to a standard
r\times 1
Gaussian random vector (if
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}=0
, the result clearly holds).

–

The term
\beta_{2}
. By
Theorem

8.2
and
4
have that

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\big\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\big\|}{\lambda_{j}}.

Again by viewing
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
as a fixed parameter, the quantity above is the product of
\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}
with a function of
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}
, which are independent. The following result makes this precise.

Lemma B.5
.

Instate the conditions of
Lemma

8.2
. With probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

Proof.

See
Section

D.4
.
∎

As a result of this lemma it holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
that

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}.

(25)

Therefore, combining the bounds in (
24
) and (
25
) we obtain that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\frac{\sigma\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}

(26)

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
.

•

Bounding
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{3}
. When it comes to
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{3}=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\lambda_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}+\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}
, we may appeal directly to the eigenvalue concentration results in
Lemma

8.2
to observe that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{3}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\lambda_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}+\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq|\lambda_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}+\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})|\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\frac{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\delta^{{\sf MD}}\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\frac{\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg|.

Therefore, it suffices to study
\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}
for
k\leq r
and
k\neq j
. The following lemma studies this error.

Lemma B.6
.

Instate the conditions of
Lemma

8.2
. Then simultaneously for all
k\neq j
, with
k,j\leq r
, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta^{{\sf MD}}}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}.

Proof.

See
Section

D.5
.
∎

Therefore,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\delta^{{\sf MD}})^{2}}{\Delta_{j}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{r}(\delta^{{\sf MD}})^{2}}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

(27)

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-9})
.

•

Bounding
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{4}
. By
Theorem

8.2
via
4
it holds that

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{4}

\displaystyle=\frac{\lambda_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}}{\lambda_{j}}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}=\frac{\lambda_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}}{\lambda_{j}}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}.

By Weyl’s inequality it holds that
|\lambda_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{n}
by
1
. Consequently,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|.

The following lemma bounds the term above in a manner similar to
Lemma

B.5
.

Lemma B.7
.

It holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\lambda_{j}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

Proof.

See
Section

D.6
.
∎

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{4}|\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}.

(28)

By (
23
), (
26
), (
27
), and (
28
) we have that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{1}+\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{2}+\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{3}+\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\sigma r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}},

where we recall that
(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}
satisfies

\displaystyle(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}.

These cumulatively hold with probability at least
1-O(n^{-9})
, which completes the proof.
∎

B.4
Proof of Lemma
8.4

First we require the following result concerning the estimated noise variance
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}
.

Lemma B.8
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

4.1
, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\sigma}}{\sigma}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}};

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}.

Proof.

See
Section

D.7
.
∎

With this in mind we may now prove
Lemma

8.4
.

Proof of
Lemma

8.4
.

We note that
Lemma

B.8
implies that with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}-b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}\big|\bigg|\sum_{l>r}\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{l}}\bigg|\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}|b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}|,

and hence
|\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}|\lesssim|b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}|=o(1)
with this same probability by
Lemma

8.1
. Therefore, by Taylor Expansion,

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}+(\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}-b_{k}^{{\sf MD}})}=\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}+O\bigg(\kappa|b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}|\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg)=\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}+O\bigg(\kappa\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg).

∎

B.5
Proof of Lemma
8.5

First we state the following result concerning the difference
(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}
.

Lemma B.9
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

4.1
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg).

Proof.

See
Section

D.8
.
∎

With these results stated we are now prepared to prove
Lemma

8.5
.

Proof of
Lemma

8.5
.

We first write the decomposition

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}-(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}

\displaystyle=2\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}}+\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}^{2}}-2\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{2}}-\frac{\sigma^{2}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle=2\big(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}\big)\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{2}}+\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+2\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg(\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}}-\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+2\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\bigg(\frac{\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}^{2}}-\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=:\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3}.

We analyze each term in turn.

•

The term
\alpha_{1}
. We note that
Lemma

B.8
demonstrates that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}+\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\asymp\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}.

•

The term
\alpha_{2}
. We note that
Lemma

B.8
demonstrates that
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}
as long as
r\lesssim n/\kappa^{2}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg(\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}}-\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{1}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}\bigg((\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})\bigg(\frac{1}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle=:\beta_{1}+\beta_{2}.

We will bound each quantity
\beta_{1}
and
\beta_{2}
separately.

–

The term
\beta_{1}
. We note that
Lemma

B.9
implies that

\displaystyle\bigg|(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}

\displaystyle(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg).

Consequently, taking a union bound over all
r
terms, we see that

\displaystyle|\beta_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\sqrt{\log(n)}\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}+(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\Bigg(\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\Bigg).

–

The term
\beta_{2}
. By the previous argument (see the proof of
Lemma

B.9
) it holds that
(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})\lesssim(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\bigg|\frac{1}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}}\bigg|.

Recall that
\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}=\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\sum_{k>r}\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}
. Moreover, by
Lemma

8.2
it holds that

\displaystyle\big|\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\lambda_{j}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\big|\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\big|+\big|\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\big|\lesssim\sigma\big(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(n)}\big)+\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{\lambda_{j}}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\big|\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\big(\sqrt{r}+\sqrt{\log(n)}\big)+\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{\lambda_{j}}+\big|\sigma^{2}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\big|\frac{n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}+\sigma\sqrt{r}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r}}{\lambda_{j}}+\sigma\sqrt{r}\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}.

This bound holds uniformly over all
j
and
k
with probability at least
1-O(n^{-8})
.
Moreover, under the assumption
\Delta_{j}\gg\sigma r\log^{2}(n)
, we have that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}+O\bigg(\frac{1}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg).

Combining these bounds results in

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\frac{1}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\lesssim(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\Bigg(\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\Bigg).

•

The term
\alpha_{3}
. Turning to
\alpha_{3}
, we observe again by
Lemma

B.8
that
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}
and hence

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg|\frac{\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}^{2}}-\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg|\frac{\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}^{2}}\bigg|+\sigma^{2}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}^{2}}\bigg|.

By a similar argument as the previous term, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}-\frac{1}{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}^{2}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{|\lambda_{j}^{3}|}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|+\frac{\sigma^{2}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{|\lambda_{j}|}.

Therefore, it suffices to analyze the difference
\bigg|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|
. First we will analyze the difference
\big|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\big|
. By orthonormality it holds that

\displaystyle\big|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\big|

\displaystyle\leq\|\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\big)\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle=\|\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg(\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)\bigg|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg(\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}.

Combining these results yields

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}.

First, if
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
, then we immediately arrive at the bound
|\alpha_{3}|\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}.
Therefore, it suffices to consider the setting that
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|>\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
. In this case we see that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}.

We will show that
\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}=O\big(\kappa^{2}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\big)
. We have that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}+\kappa^{2}\max_{k\neq j}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}.

Together, this analysis reveals that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|\lesssim\begin{cases}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}&\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|;\\
\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\kappa^{2}(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}})^{2}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}&\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.\end{cases}

Combining our bounds for
\alpha_{1},\alpha_{2}
and
\alpha_{3}
, we have that

\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}-\big(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\Bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\Bigg),

for
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
. In addition, for
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|<\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
.

\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}-\big(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\Bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\kappa^{2}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}\Bigg).

The proof is therefore completed by noting that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\frac{\frac{\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}}{\big(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}}-1}{\frac{\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}+1}\Bigg|\lesssim\frac{1}{(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}}\big|\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}-\big(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\big|.

∎

B.6
Proof of Lemma
8.6

Proof.

We need to show that each term is
o(1)
. First, we recall that we assume that

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}\geq C_{0}\sigma\kappa^{2}r\sqrt{n}\log^{3/2}(n);\qquad\Delta_{\min}\geq C_{0}\sigma r^{3/2}\log^{5/2}(n).

These two assumptions immediately imply that

\displaystyle{\sf ErrMD}=\frac{r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}/\sigma}+\frac{r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}/\sigma}+n^{-9}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\log(n)}=o(1);

\displaystyle\frac{\kappa\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}=o(1).

Next, recall that we assume that

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)=o(1).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}}{\sf ErrBiasMD}

\displaystyle=\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=o(1).

In addition, this assumption further implies that

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\frac{\kappa\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}=o(1).

Therefore, it suffices to demonstrate that
\sqrt{\log(n)}{\sf ErrCIMD}=o(1)
. Recall that
{\sf ErrCIMD}
has two definitions based on whether
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
or not (see
Lemma

8.5
). We consider each case separately.

•

Case 1
:
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|<\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
. Under this condition it holds that

\displaystyle{\sf ErrCIMD}=\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf MD}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}.

(29)

Recall that we assume that
r\leq c_{0}\frac{n}{\kappa^{2}\log^{2}(n)}
, which ensures that
\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big)
. In addition, our assumption on
s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf MD}}/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}
implies that

\displaystyle\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf MD}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big),

Similarly, it is straightforward to verify that
\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big)
under our assumption
\lambda_{\min}\geq C_{0}\sigma\kappa^{2}r\sqrt{n}\log^{3/2}(n)
. For the final term, we note that when
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|<\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
we further assume that
|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|
which implies that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa^{2}\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big).

Therefore,
{\sf ErrCIMD}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big)
.

•

Case 2
:
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\geq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
. In this setting, the only difference in
{\sf ErrCIMD}
in (
29
) is the final term, which is of the form
\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big)
by our signal-strength assumption.

Combining these results completes the proof.
∎

B.7
Proof of Corollary
3.1

Proof.

The proof proceeds by slightly modifying the proof of
Theorem

3.2
. First, by
Lemma

8.1
with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\big|1-\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}.

Therefore, for any
z\in\mathbb{R}
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\big)\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big)\leq z+\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}(\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-1)\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big)\leq z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|+O(n^{-10})

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big)\leq z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\Phi(z)-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)\bigg|+O(n^{-10})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}/\sigma}+\frac{r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}/\sigma}+n^{-9}+\bigg|\Phi(z)-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)\bigg)\bigg|

(30)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}/\sigma}+\frac{r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}/\sigma}+n^{-9}+\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)

(31)

where (
30
) is due to
Theorem

3.1
and the last line is due to the Lipschitz property of
\Phi(\cdot)
. We now analyze the remaining term.

First, we note that
s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}
satisfies the lower bound

\displaystyle s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}\geq\max\bigg\{\frac{\sigma}{\Delta_{j}}\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|,\frac{\sigma\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{|\lambda_{j}|}\bigg\},

where the first term arises by taking
k
such that
|\lambda_{k}-\lambda_{j}|=\Delta_{j}
. Therefore, the remaining term in (
31
) satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}n}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}\frac{\sigma n}{|\lambda_{j}|}+\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}\frac{\sigma r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{|\lambda_{j}|}+\frac{\sigma r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}},

where the final bound follows from the assumption
\max_{k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\lesssim n^{-1/2}
, which implies that
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\gtrsim 1
, as well as the assumption
|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|\lesssim|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|
. It is readily seen that this final bound is smaller than the first two terms in (
31
), which completes the proof.
∎

B.8
Proof of Corollary
4.1

Proof.

The proof is essentially the same as
Theorem

4.1
. Observe that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\widehat{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}}\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\leq z+\bigg(\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(1-\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\bigg)\bigg)\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|.

We now note that it is straightforward to verify that the proof of
Corollary

3.1
reveals that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\log(n)},

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-8})
, and as a consequence, with this same probability,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(1-\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\log(n)}{\sf ErrCIMD}

by
Lemma

8.5
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\widehat{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}}\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\leq z\pm{\sf ErrCIMD}\sqrt{\log(n)}\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|+O(n^{-8})

(32)

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\leq z\pm{\sf ErrCIMD}\sqrt{\log(n)}\bigg\}-\Phi\bigg(z\pm{\sf ErrCIMD}\sqrt{\log(n)}\bigg)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+{\sf ErrCIMD}\sqrt{\log(n)}+O(n^{-8})

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}/\sigma}+\frac{r^{3/2}\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}/\sigma}+n^{-8}+{\sf ErrCIMD}\sqrt{\log(n)},

where we have applied
Corollary

3.1
in the final line, and (
32
) follows from
Lemma

8.5
. Therefore, the result holds if we can show that
{\sf ErrCIMD}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big)
(from
Lemma

8.5
) under our assumptions.

Recall that
{\sf ErrCIMD}
takes on two values based on whether
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|\geq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|
or not. Under the assumption
\max_{k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\lesssim n^{-1/2}
it holds that
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|\geq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|
. Therefore, it suffices to demonstrate that

\displaystyle{\sf ErrCIMD}:=\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}+\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{e}_{i},k}^{{\sf MD}}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big).

The proof of
Lemma

8.6
reveals that our signal-strength assumptions imply that each term above except
\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{e}_{i},k}^{{\sf MD}}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}
is
o(1)
. Therefore, it suffices to show that this remaining term is
o(1)
.

First, we note that

\displaystyle\max_{k\neq j}\frac{(s_{\bm{e}_{i},k}^{{\sf MD}})^{2}}{(s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}})^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf MD}})^{2}}\bigg(\frac{r\sigma^{2}\max_{1\leq l\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}|^{2}}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\sigma^{2}\Delta_{j}^{2}}{n\Delta_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\max_{1\leq l\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}|^{2}}{\min_{1\leq k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}}{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\Delta_{j}^{2}r}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\kappa^{2},

where the final line follows from the observation that
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|^{2}\asymp 1
and the assumption
|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\asymp|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}|
for all
k,l\leq r
. Therefore, the result holds as long as

\displaystyle\frac{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{r}}{\Delta_{\min}}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}+\kappa\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{j}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma\kappa r\sqrt{\log(n)}}{\Delta_{\min}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n)}}\big).

Our assumption on
\Delta_{\min}
implies that
\Delta_{\min}/\sigma\gtrsim r^{3/2}\kappa\log^{5/2}(n)
, which give the result. ∎

Appendix C
Proofs of PCA Lemmas and Corollaries

In this section we prove all the preliminary results from
Section

8.3
as well as the corollaries in
Sections

3.2
and
4.2

C.1
Proof of Lemma
8.8

Proof.

We note that our assumptions imply the noise assumption in
Li et al. 2022
, since our condition on
r
implies the noise condition (as demonstrated immediately after
5
).
Therefore, the first inequality holds by equation 5.79 in
Li et al. 2022
.

As for the second inequality, we note that equation 5.81 of
Li et al. 2022
implies that

\displaystyle\big|

\displaystyle 1-\sqrt{1+b_{j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{cases}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}n}&p>n\\
\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\bigg((\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}+(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg)&p\leq n\end{cases}

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. We claim that each the bounds above are less than the claimed bound in the statement of
Lemma

8.8
. Therefore, it suffices to show that for
p>n
that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\Bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Bigg),

and that for
p\leq n
it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\bigg((\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}+(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\Bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Bigg).

We consider each case separately.

•

Case 1:
p>n
. For
p>n
the assumption (
4
) implies that
\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}\lesssim\lambda_{\min}
, which in particular implies that
\sigma^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{\min}
. Therefore, using the fact that
\frac{\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2}}\leq\kappa
,

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{pr\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}n}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}\lambda_{\max}^{1/2}\sqrt{\kappa pr\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\kappa pr\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}n}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\frac{\kappa\sqrt{rp\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\Bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\kappa r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Bigg).

•

Case 2:
p\leq n
.
When
p\leq n
we observe that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\bigg((\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}+(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\sqrt{p}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\kappa r\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}}\frac{p}{n}+\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}^{1/2}}\frac{p}{n}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\kappa r\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}}\frac{p}{n}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\bigg(\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\kappa r\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

(33)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\kappa\sigma\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\kappa r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

∎

C.2
Proof of Lemma
8.9

Let
\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)
denote the matrix

\displaystyle\bm{\widetilde{G}}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)

\displaystyle:=\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U};

\displaystyle\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)

\displaystyle:=\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}},

where we recall
\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}=\bm{\Lambda}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{r}
.
The following lemma studies these two terms.

Lemma C.1
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

3.3
, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\in[2(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3,4(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3]}\|\bm{\widetilde{G}}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)-\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)\|\lesssim(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log^{2}(n\vee p).

In addition,

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\in[2(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3,4(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3]}\|\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}.

Proof.

This follows from the argument en route to the proof of Theorem 8 of
Li et al. 2022
(see their equation 5.57), together with their bound 5.61, replacing the terms
\log(n)
with
\log(n\vee p)
throughout.
∎

Proof of
Lemma

8.9
.

The perturbation bounds follows from Theorem 8 of
Li et al. 2022
where we note that their assumptions are slightly weaker than our assumptions by factors of
r
and
\log(p\vee n)/\log(n)
.

We therefore study the approximation of
\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})
to
\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})
. Let
\bm{Z}=\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{X}
, and suppose that
\bm{ZZ}^{\top}/n
has eigenvalues
\lambda_{k}^{\perp}
. Then observe that

\displaystyle\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}.

By the Poincare Separation Theorem (Corollary 4.3.37 in
Horn and Johnson 2012
) it holds that

\displaystyle\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}\leq\lambda_{k}^{\perp}\leq\widehat{\lambda}_{k}

for all
1\leq k\leq p-r
. In addition, note that the function
x\mapsto\frac{x}{a-x}
has derivative
\frac{a}{(a-x)^{2}}
which is strictly nonnegative for
a
positive, and hence the function
\frac{x}{a-x}
is increasing in
x
for
x\leq a
and
a
positive. By
6
it holds that
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\in[2\lambda_{j}/3,4\lambda_{j}/3]
and hence is positive. In addition, by
5
, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,
\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}\leq\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
, and similarly
\lambda_{k}^{\perp}\leq\widehat{\lambda}_{j}
and hence

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}}\leq\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}\leq\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}

for each
1\leq k\leq\min(p-r,n)
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=r+1}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=r+1}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}.

In addition,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}

\displaystyle\geq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k+r}}\geq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k={r+1}}^{\min(p,n+r)}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}\geq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k={r+1}}^{\min(p-r,n)}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k=1}^{r}\frac{\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}^{\perp}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r}{n\lambda_{j}}\lambda_{1}^{\perp}

We therefore bound
\lambda_{1}^{\perp}
. However, we note that by
\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}
is the empirical covariance for i.i.d. standard Gaussians. Consequently, by the covariance concentration inequality for Gaussian random variables (e.g.,
Lemma

8.7
),

\displaystyle\lambda_{1}^{\perp}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\|+\sigma^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(1+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}\bigg),

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. Therefore, with this same probability,

\displaystyle\bigg|\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{n\lambda_{j}}\sigma^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}\bigg)

which completes the proof.
∎

C.3
Proof of Lemma
8.10

Proof.

We bound each residual in turn.

•

Bounding
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{1}
.
We first decompose
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{1}
in a similar manner to
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf MD}}_{1}
via

\displaystyle\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=:\alpha_{1}+\alpha_{2}+\alpha_{3},

where we have implicitly invoked
Theorem

8.2
via
7
.
We bound
\alpha_{1}
through
\alpha_{3}
sequentially.

–

The term
\alpha_{1}
. By Cauchy-Schwarz,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j})^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{u}_{j}\|\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|,

which holds with probabilty at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
, where in the final line we have implicitly invoked
Lemma

8.8
through a similar argument as in the matrix denoising setting. The following lemma bounds the remaining term above.

Lemma C.2
.

Instate the conditions of
Theorem

3.3
. Then with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

Proof.

See
Section

E.1
.
∎

As a consequence, we obtain that with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
.

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}r\sqrt{\kappa\log(n\vee p)}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}.

(34)

–

The term
\alpha_{2}
. When it comes to
\alpha_{2}
we note that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{2}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}.

The following lemma bounds this additional term.

Lemma C.3
.

Instate the conditions in
Theorem

3.1
. Then with probability at least

it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log^{2}(n\vee p).

Proof.

See
Section

E.2
.
∎

Therefore, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{l}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}.

\alpha_{3}

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}.

First, the following lemma demonstrates that the first term concentrates well.

Lemma C.4

. Then with probability at least

E.3
.

Therefore,

. Observe that

Bounding

. We decompose via

).

Lemma C.7
.

Instate the conditions in

. Then simultaneously for all

, with probability at least

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{|\lambda_{k}-\lambda_{j}|}.

.

Proof.

See
Section

E.6
.
∎

As a result of this lemma,

\displaystyle|\eta_{1}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\sqrt{n}\Delta_{j}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta^{{\sf PCA}}\sqrt{\kappa r\log(n\vee p)}}{\sqrt{n}\Delta_{j}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta^{{\sf PCA}}r\sqrt{\kappa\log(n\vee p)}}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}},

(41)

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-9})
.

–

The term
\eta_{2}
.
Lemma

8.9
guarantees that
\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}
with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
.
Consequently,

\displaystyle|\eta_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}||\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\kappa r\log(n\vee p)}\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}

\displaystyle\lesssim r\sqrt{\kappa\log(n\vee p)}\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}},

(42)

where we have implicitly invoked
Lemma

C.7
in the first line. Therefore, the inequality (
42
) holds with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-9})
.

Therefore, combining (
41
) and (
42
) we have that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim r\sqrt{\kappa\log(n\vee p)}\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}+\frac{\delta^{{\sf PCA}}}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

(43)

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-9})
.

•

Bounding
\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{4}
. By Weyl’s inequality,
Lemma

8.9
,
Theorem

8.2
, and
7
it holds that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{4}|=\bigg|\frac{\widetilde{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\lambda}_{j}}{\lambda_{j}}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{j}}|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{j}}|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|.

The following lemma provides a bound for this remaining quantity.

Lemma C.8
.

Instate the conditions in
Theorem

3.3
. Then with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma\sqrt{r}\log(n\vee p)}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{j}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

Proof.

see
Section

E.7
.
∎

As a consequence of this lemma, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}\sqrt{r\kappa\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{j}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

(44)

We now complete the proof. By (
37
), (
40
), (
43
), (
44
) with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{1}

\displaystyle+\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{2}+\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{3}+\mathcal{R}^{{\sf PCA}}_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\Bigg(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}r\sqrt{\kappa\log(n\vee p)}+\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\Bigg)\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r\kappa}\log(n\vee p)\Bigg(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\Bigg)\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad+r\sqrt{\kappa\log(n\vee p)}\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{j}n}+\frac{\delta^{{\sf PCA}}}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg)\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}\sqrt{r\kappa\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma}{\sqrt{n}\lambda_{j}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)+\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{j}}+\frac{\delta^{{\sf PCA}}}{\Delta_{j}}\Bigg)s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}

\displaystyle=:{\sf\widetilde{ErrPCA}}\times s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}

where we recall that

\displaystyle(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})+\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{n\lambda_{j}^{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}.

We now bound
{\sf\widetilde{ErrPCA}}
. Plugging in the definition of
\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}
from (
13
),
\delta^{{\sf PCA}}
from (
14
), and
\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}
from (
12
), we see that

\displaystyle{\sf\widetilde{ErrPCA}}

\displaystyle\asymp\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log(n\vee p)}{\sqrt{n}\Delta_{j}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)+\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma\sqrt{p}\log(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{j}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\log(n\vee p)+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{j}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa r\log^{2}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\log(n\vee p)+\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}

\displaystyle\quad\qquad\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sigma\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\log(n\vee p)\sqrt{\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2}}}{\lambda_{j}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\kappa r^{5/2}\log^{5/2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}\log^{5/2}(n\vee p)}{\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa^{3/2}r\log^{3}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)+\frac{\sigma}{\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=:{{\sf ErrPCA}},

which matches the bound in
Theorem

3.3
, where we slightly overload notation by including the implicit constants in
ErrPCA
.
∎

C.4
Proof of Lemma
8.11

Proof.

Recalling that
\bm{X}_{i}=\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{Y}_{i}
where
\bm{Y}_{i}
are i.i.d
\mathcal{N}(0,\bm{I}_{p})
random variables, we observe that

\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq j}

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg).

Consider each summand above. By independence of
\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}
and
\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}
for
k\neq j
, we can condition on
\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}
to observe that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}

\displaystyle\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\sim\mathcal{N}\big(0,(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}})^{2}\big),

where

\displaystyle\big(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i})^{2}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}+\|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i})^{2}.

Therefore, conditional on
\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}
,

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{1}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\sim\mathcal{N}\bigg(0,(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}})^{2}\frac{(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}}{(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}})^{2}}\bigg).

Consequently,

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}

\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\leq z\ \bigg|\ \bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bigg\}-\Phi(z)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\leq z\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\ \bigg|\ \bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bigg\}-\Phi(z)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\leq z\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\ \bigg|\ \bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bigg\}-\Phi\bigg(z\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\Phi\bigg(z\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg)-\Phi(z)

\displaystyle=\Phi\bigg(z\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg)-\Phi(z).

(45)

In addition, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\big(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big)^{2}}{(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}(\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i})^{2}-1\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}},

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
by Bernstein’s inequality. As a consequence, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{1+\frac{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg|\frac{\big(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big)^{2}}{(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}}-1\bigg|\lesssim\bigg|\frac{\big(\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big)^{2}}{(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}}-1\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}.

(46)

Let
\mathcal{E}:=\{\eqref{eventholds1}\text{ holds}\}
. Then by (
45
) on the event
\mathcal{E}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\leq z\ \bigg|\ \bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\Phi\bigg(z\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg)-\Phi(z)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\frac{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\widetilde{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}-1\bigg|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}},

(47)

where we have used the Lipschitz property of the Gaussian cumulative distributional function.

We are now prepared to complete the proof. Define

\displaystyle\xi:=\sum_{k\neq j}\frac{\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}.

Then it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\leq z\bigg\}
