\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}_{\xi/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq z}|\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\right]\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}_{\xi/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq z}\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}}\mid\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\right]\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}_{\xi/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq z}\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}}\mid\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\right]\right].

(48)

Note that
\mathcal{E}
depends only on
\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}
. Therefore, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}_{\xi/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq z}\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}}\mid\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{I}_{\xi/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq z}\mid\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}}\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\xi/s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq z\mid\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bigg\}=\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}}\Bigg(\Phi(z)+O\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\Bigg),

where the final inequality holds by (
47
). Plugging this into (
48
) it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\xi}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\bigg[\Phi(z)\mathbb{I}_{\mathcal{E}^{c}}\bigg]+O\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim(n\vee p)^{-10}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}},

where the final inequality holds since
\Phi(z)\leq 1
and
\mathbb{P}\big\{\mathcal{E}^{c}\big\}\lesssim(n\vee p)^{-10}
. This completes the proof.
∎

C.5
Proof of Lemma
8.12

First we state the following lemma concerning the estimate of the noise variance.

Lemma C.9
.

Denote, for
n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)<p
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}^{2}:

\displaystyle=\frac{{\sf{Tr}}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{r}\big)}{p-r},

where
\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{r}
is the rank
r
approximation of
\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}
, and set for
n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)\geq p
,

\displaystyle\widehat{\sigma}^{2}:=\widehat{\lambda}_{r+1}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\big).

Then with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\sigma},

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}:

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\sqrt{r}\kappa\frac{\log^{4}(n\vee p)}{n}+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}&n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)<p;\\
\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(p\vee n)}{n}}&n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)\geq p.\end{cases}

Proof.

See
Section

E.8
.
∎

We are now prepared to prove
Lemma

8.12
.

Proof of
Lemma

8.12
.

Observe that
\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}=b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}
when
n\geq p
; therefore, it suffices to consider the setting that
n<p
. In this case, by
Lemma

B.8
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}=o(\sigma^{2})

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. Let
\Delta_{\sigma}=\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}
. Then

\displaystyle\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}.

We claim that

\displaystyle\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}=b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}+O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)=b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}+o(1).

(49)

If this is indeed the case, then

\displaystyle\bigg|\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}-\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg),

which follows from the fact that
|b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{k}n}=o(1)
. Therefore, the result will be proven using the bound for
\Delta_{\sigma}
. It therefore suffices to demonstrate that (
49
) holds. Therefore, it suffices to demonstrate that one has

\displaystyle\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}+O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg),

(50)

as well as that

\displaystyle\frac{(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg).

(51)

Note that with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,
\Delta_{\sigma}=o(\sigma^{2})
, and hence
\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}=o(1)
with this same probability by the noise assumption (
4
). Henceforth we fix this event. We now prove (
50
) and (
51
) sequentially.

•

Showing (
50
)
. Denote for convenience
C=\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}
which does not depend on
\Delta_{\sigma}
. We then have that

\displaystyle C\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle=C\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}

Observe that by Taylor Series,

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\sum_{n=1}^{\infty}\bigg[\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\bigg]^{n}

\displaystyle=\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\tfrac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\tfrac{p}{n}}}{1-\tfrac{\Delta_{\sigma}\tfrac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\tfrac{p}{n}}}

where the final series expansion converges by
6
together with the fact that
\lambda_{\min}\gg\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}
. Plugging this into (
) we have that

\displaystyle C\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}

\displaystyle=C\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+C\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\tfrac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\tfrac{p}{n}}}{1-\tfrac{\Delta_{\sigma}\tfrac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\tfrac{p}{n}}}.

We note that

\displaystyle\bigg|C\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\tfrac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\tfrac{p}{n}}}{1-\tfrac{\Delta_{\sigma}\tfrac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\tfrac{p}{n}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}^{2}}{n|\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}||\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}|}\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\lesssim\frac{1}{n}\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}},

where the final inequality holds by
6
and the noise assumption (
4
) (which implies that
\sigma^{2}=o(\lambda_{\min})
), and hence that
\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{|\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}|}\lesssim 1
and that
\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\lesssim 1
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}

\displaystyle\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\big(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma}\big)\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}+\sum_{r<i\leq n}O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{n\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{n+\sum_{r<i\leq n}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i}}}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{i}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\widehat{\lambda}_{i})^{2}}+O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg),

which proves (
50
).

•

Showing (
51
)
. We simply proceed by Taylor expansion to observe that

\displaystyle\frac{(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-(\sigma^{2}+\Delta_{\sigma})\frac{p}{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+O\bigg(\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}\frac{p}{n}}{(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n})^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p}{n}}+O\bigg(\frac{\Delta_{\sigma}\frac{p}{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg),

where we have again used the noise assumption (
4
) and
6
. This proves (
51
).

∎

C.6
Proof of Lemma
8.13

We first state the following result concerning the difference
(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}
.

Lemma C.10
.

Under the conditions of
Theorem

4.2
, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-8})
the following holds:

\displaystyle\bigg|(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}}\log(n\vee p)+|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|^{2}\mathcal{E}_{b}.

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{b}:

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{2}r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}n}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{n}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)&\\
\qquad+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\bigg(\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)\Bigg)&p>n;\\
\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{2}r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}n}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{n}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)&p\leq n.\end{cases}

Proof.

See
Section

E.9
.
∎

With this result stated we are now prepared to prove
Lemma

8.13
.

Proof of
Lemma

8.13
.

Motivated by the decomposition in the proof of
Lemma

8.5
in
Section

B.5
, it holds that

\displaystyle\big(\widehat{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big)^{2}-(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})+\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}(\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}-\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}_{\alpha_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}-\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}_{\alpha_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}}_{\alpha_{3}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg)}_{\alpha_{4}}.

We will bound
\alpha_{1}
through
\alpha_{4}
in turn. At the outset we make note of the following consequences of
Lemmas

8.9
and
C.9
. For each
k\in[r]
with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\big|\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{1+\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k})}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\big|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{1+\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k})+(\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k})-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k}))}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{1+\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k})}\big|\widehat{\gamma}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k})-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{k})\big|+\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{k}}{1+\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}r}{n\lambda_{k}}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}\bigg)+(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p).

(53)

As a consequence,
\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}\lesssim\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2}
, and
|\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}|\gtrsim|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|.

In addition, by
Lemma

C.9
, with this same probability

\displaystyle\big|\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\lambda_{j}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\sigma^{2}\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}.

As a result,

\displaystyle\big|\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\geq\lambda_{j}+o(\lambda_{j})\gtrsim\lambda_{j},

which follows from the noise assumption (
4
) and the definition of
\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}
. Finally, by
Lemma

C.9
it holds that
\widehat{\sigma}^{2}\lesssim\sigma^{2}
with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. Armed with these results we are now prepared to bound
\alpha_{1}
through
\alpha_{4}
.

•

The term
\alpha_{1}
.

For a given
k\in[r]
we have that

\displaystyle\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})n}\bigg|(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}\bigg|.

Lemma

C.10
implies that with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-8})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}}\log(n\vee p)+|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|^{2}\mathcal{E}_{b}.

As a result,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{1}|

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}\bigg(|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\log(n\vee p)+|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|^{2}\mathcal{E}_{b}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\log(n\vee p)\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|+\mathcal{E}_{b}(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})\sqrt{n}}|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}|+\mathcal{E}_{b}(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\Bigg(\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\mathcal{E}_{b}\Bigg).

•

The term
\alpha_{2}
.

For a given
k\in[r]
, it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}-\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\frac{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}-\sigma^{2})\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}-\lambda_{k}-\sigma^{2})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}-\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|.

By the fact that
|\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}-\lambda_{k}-\sigma^{2}|\lesssim(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)
, the first two terms above satisfy

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}-\sigma^{2})\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|+\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}-\lambda_{k}-\sigma^{2})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}.

For the remaining term, by Taylor expansion we have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k})^{2}n}-\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}n}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{n}\frac{|\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widecheck{\lambda}_{k}-\lambda_{j}-\sigma^{2}+\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2}|}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|^{3}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}{n(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p).

As a consequence, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\bigg)(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}.

•

The term
\alpha_{3}
.
By decomposing we have that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\underbrace{\bigg|\frac{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}-\sigma^{2})\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\bigg|}_{\beta_{1}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}+\underbrace{\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})(\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2})}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\bigg|}_{\beta_{2}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}-\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\bigg|}_{\beta_{3}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}.

We bound
\beta_{1}
,
\beta_{2}
, and
\beta_{3}
in turn.

–

Bounding
\beta_{1}
. By the eigenvalue bounds in (
53
), with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\beta_{1}

\displaystyle=\bigg|\frac{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}-\sigma^{2})\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\bigg|\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}.

–

Bounding
\beta_{2}
. By
Lemma

C.9
, it holds that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\sigma^{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|\beta_{2}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}.

–

Bounding
\beta_{3}
. Finally, for
\beta_{3}
, we note that

\displaystyle\big|\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2}-\lambda_{j}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\sigma^{2}\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}=\lambda_{j}\big(1+o(1)\big).

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}-\frac{1}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}^{3}}\bigg((\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\sigma^{2}\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\bigg),

and hence

\displaystyle\beta_{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\bigg(\big(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\big)\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\bigg).

Therefore, combining these bounds, we obtain that with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{3}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\bigg)

•

The term
\alpha_{4}
.
We observe that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{4}|

\displaystyle=\frac{\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{(\widecheck{\lambda}_{j}-\widehat{\sigma}^{2})^{2}n}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\bigg|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|.

It suffices then to focus on the difference
\bigg|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|
. First consider
\big|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\big|
. By orthonormality, we have that

\displaystyle\big|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\big|

\displaystyle=\big|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\big|

\displaystyle\leq\|\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}-\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big)\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle=\|\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg),

where the final inequality holds on the event in
5
by the Davis-Kahan Theorem.
Consequently,

\displaystyle\bigg|\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}-\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg(\|\bm{\widehat{U}}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)\bigg(\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}.

We therefore have that

\displaystyle|\alpha_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\bigg).

If
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
, then it holds that
|\alpha_{3}|\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}
. Therefore, it suffices to consider when
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|>\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
. In this case,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\frac{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa^{2}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\big(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle|\alpha_{4}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\begin{cases}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}&\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|;\\
\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\kappa^{2}(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j})^{2}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}&\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.\end{cases}

Combining all of our bounds we arrive at the bound

\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}-\big(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\Bigg(\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\bigg)+\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\mathcal{E}_{b}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\Bigg)

if
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
and

\displaystyle\big|\big(\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}}\big)^{2}-\big(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\big(s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},j}\big)^{2}\Bigg(\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\bigg)+\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})}{\Delta_{j}}\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\mathcal{E}_{b}+\kappa^{2}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j})^{2}}{(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}\Bigg),

both of which hold with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-8})
, which completes the proof.
∎

C.7
Proof of Lemma
8.14

Proof.

We consider each term separately.

•

The quantity
{\sf ErrPCA}
. First, recall that we have the assumptions

\displaystyle\lambda_{\min}

\displaystyle\geq C_{1}\max\bigg\{\sigma^{2}\kappa^{3}r^{2}\log^{7}(n\vee p)\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg),\sigma\kappa\log^{4}(n\vee p)\frac{p}{n}\bigg\};

\displaystyle\Delta_{\min}

\displaystyle\geq C_{1}\big(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\frac{r^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\log^{4}(n\vee p),

(54)

where
C_{1}
is some sufficiently large constant. Therefore,

\displaystyle{\sf ErrPCA}

\displaystyle=\kappa^{3/2}r\log^{3}(n\vee p)\Bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)+\frac{\sigma}{\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa r^{5/2}\log^{5/2}(n\vee p)\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}\log^{5/2}(n\vee p)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}+\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}\log^{5/2}(n\vee p)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}.

In addition, we assume that
r^{3}\leq c_{1}\frac{n}{\kappa^{6}\log^{12}(n\vee p)}
, where
c_{1}
is a sufficiently small constant, which implies that

\displaystyle\kappa^{2}r^{3/2}\log^{5/2}(n\vee p)\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}},

thereby ensuring that
{\sf ErrPCA}=o(1)
.

•

The quantity
{\sf ErrBiasPCA}
. Recall that we assume that

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}

\displaystyle\Bigg(\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad\qquad+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\frac{\sqrt{r}\kappa\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}{n}\Bigg)=o(1),

(55)

which, from the definition of
{\sf ErrBiasPCA}
in
Theorem

3.2
directly ensures that

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\sf ErrBiasPCA}=o(1).

•

The quantity
{\sf ErrCIPCA}
.
Next we consider
\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIPCA}
(defined in
Lemma

8.13
). Recall that
{\sf ErrCIPCA}
takes on two separate definitions based on whether
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|<\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
or not. First assume that
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\leq\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
. Then

\displaystyle{\sf ErrCIPCA}

\displaystyle=\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)+\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\bigg)+\bigg(1+\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\bigg)\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathcal{E}_{b}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}

\displaystyle:=\begin{cases}\sqrt{r}\kappa\frac{\log^{4}(n\vee p)}{n}+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}&n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)<p;\\
\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(p\vee n)}{n}}&n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)\geq p.\end{cases}

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{b}:

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{2}r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}n}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{n}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)&\\
\qquad+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\bigg(\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)\Bigg)&p>n;\\
\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{2}r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}n}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{n}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)&p\leq n.\end{cases}

It is straightforward to demonstrate that since we assume that

\displaystyle\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}=o(1),

it holds that the last three terms are
o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big)
by the noise assumptions (
54
). In addition, it is straightforward to show that the noise assumption and the condition on
r
implies that

\displaystyle\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log(n\vee p)=o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big).

Therefore, it suffices to show that
\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}=o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big)
. Since
\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}
takes two forms based on whether
n/\log^{2}(n\vee p)<p
or not, we consider each case separately.

–

Case 1:
n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)<p
. In this case
\sigma^{2}\lesssim\lambda_{j}
since
\lambda_{\min}/\sigma^{2}\gtrsim\log^{3}(n\vee p)\frac{p}{n}\gtrsim\log(n\vee p)
. Therefore, we have that

\displaystyle\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}\lesssim\frac{\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{4}(n\vee p)}{n}+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\lesssim n^{-5/6}+\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big),

where the penultimate inequality follows from the assumption

\displaystyle r^{3}\leq c_{0}\frac{n}{\kappa^{6}\log^{12}(n\vee p)}

as well as the assumption
\log(p)\lesssim\sqrt{n}
.

–

Case 2:
p\leq n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)
. In this case we have that

\displaystyle\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\mathcal{E}_{\sigma}

\displaystyle=\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg).

If
\sigma^{2}\geq\lambda_{\min}
, then

\displaystyle\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)=o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big)

by the noise assumption (
54
). However, if
\sigma^{2}<\lambda_{\min}
, then we have that

\displaystyle\bigg(1+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}=o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big)

since
p/n=o\big(\frac{1}{\log^{4}(n\vee p)}\big)
and
\log(n\vee p)=\log(n)
.

•

The quantity
{\sf ErrBiasApproxPCA}
. Finally we consider
{\sf ErrBiasApproxPCA}
(defined in
Eq.

15
). Recall that we need to demonstrate that
\bigg(\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}+\log(n\vee p)\bigg){\sf ErrBiasApproxPCA}=o(1).
When
p\leq n
we are done since
{\sf ErrbiasApproxPCA}=0
.
Therefore, it suffices to consider the setting that
p>n
. In this case we have that

\displaystyle{\sf ErrBiasApproxPCA}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)}_{\alpha_{1}}+\underbrace{\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}}_{\alpha_{2}}.

We consider
\alpha_{1}
and
\alpha_{2}
separately.

–

The term
\alpha_{1}
. We have that since
p>n
,

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)\lesssim\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\sqrt{r}\kappa\frac{\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}{n}=o(1)

by (
55
). In addition, our noise assumption (
54
) directly implies that
{\sf ErrBiasApproxPCA}=o(\log^{-2}(n\vee p))
.

–

The term
\alpha_{2}
. By considering the noise assumption (
54
), it is straightforward to demonstrate that
\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}/\lambda_{\min}\lesssim\frac{1}{\log^{2}(n\vee p)}
(e.g., see the discussion following
5
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}+\log(n\vee p)\bigg)\alpha_{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}+\log(n\vee p)\bigg)\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p\log^{2}(n\vee p)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}+\log(n\vee p)\bigg)\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\frac{\sqrt{r}}{n\log^{2}(n\vee p)}=o(1),

which follows from (
54
) and (
55
).

This completes the proof.
∎

C.8
Proof of Corollary
3.2

Proof.

We follow the proof of
Corollary

3.1
in
Section

B.7
. by
Lemma

8.8
it holds that

\displaystyle|1-\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|\lesssim\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2},

where
\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}
is defined in
Eq.

13
. Therefore, for any
z\in\mathbb{R}
we have that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\big)\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|

\displaystyle=\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big)\leq z+\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}(\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-1)\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big)\leq z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|+O(n^{-10})

\displaystyle\leq\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{1}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}\bm{u}_{j}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}\big)\leq z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\bigg\}-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\bigg)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\Phi(z)-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\bigg)\bigg|+O(n^{-10})

\displaystyle\lesssim{\sf ErrPCA}+\bigg|\Phi(z)-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}\bigg)\bigg|

(56)

\displaystyle\lesssim{\sf ErrPCA}+\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}

(57)

where (
56
) is due to
Theorem

3.3
and the last line is due to the Lipschitz property of
\Phi(\cdot)
. We now analyze the remaining term.

First, we note that
s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}
satisfies the lower bound

\displaystyle s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\geq\max\bigg\{\frac{(\lambda_{\min}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|,\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\bigg\},

where the first term arises by taking
k
such that
|\lambda_{k}-\lambda_{j}|=\Delta_{j}
. In addition,

\displaystyle\big(\mathcal{F}^{{\sf PCA}}\big)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}n}+\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}p\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}.

Therefore, the remaining term in (
57
) satisfies

\displaystyle\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}

\displaystyle\bigg(\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}^{2}n}+\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}p\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}^{2}n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}\kappa\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma p\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|}{|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|}\frac{\kappa(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma\sqrt{p}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\kappa(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}+\bigg(\frac{\sigma}{\sqrt{\lambda_{j}}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg)\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\log^{2}(n\vee p),

where the final bound follows from the assumption
\max_{k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\lesssim p^{-1/2}
, which implies that
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\gtrsim 1
, as well as the assumption
|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}|\asymp|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|
. It is straightforward to check that this quantity is strictly less than
{\sf ErrPCA}
, which completes the proof.
∎

C.9
Proof of Corollary
4.2

Proof.

We follow a similar proof to
Corollary

4.1
in
Section

B.8
. We have that

\displaystyle\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\widehat{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|=\Bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\leq z+\bigg(\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(1-\frac{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\bigg)\bigg)\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\Bigg|.

We now note that it is straightforward to verify that the proof of
Corollary

3.1
reveals that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg|\lesssim\log(n\vee p),

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-8})
, and as a consequence, with this same probability,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}\bigg(1-\frac{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\bigg)\bigg|\lesssim\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIPCA}

by
Lemma

8.13
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\mathbb{P}

\displaystyle\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\leq z\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\leq z\pm\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIMD}\bigg\}-\Phi(z)\bigg|+O((n\vee p)^{-8})

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\frac{\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}-\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{j}}{\widehat{s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{e}_{i},j}}}\leq z\pm\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIMD}\bigg\}-\Phi\bigg(z\pm\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIMD}\bigg\}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\Phi\bigg(z\pm\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIMD}\bigg\}\bigg)-\Phi(z)\bigg|+O((n\vee p)^{-8})

\displaystyle\lesssim{\sf ErrPCA}+\log(n\vee p){\sf ErrCIPCA},

where the final line follows from
Corollary

3.2
(as our assumptions are strictly stronger than those therein), the pneultimate line is due the Lipschitz property of
\Phi
. Therefore, the result is completed provided we can show that
{\sf ErrCIPCA}=o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big)
under our assumptions.

First, recall that
{\sf ErrCIPCA}
takes on two definitions depending on whether
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\geq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
or not. However, under our assumption
\max_{k}\|\bm{u}_{k}\|_{\infty}\lesssim p^{-1/2}
, we have that
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|\gtrsim 1
under the assumption
r\leq p^{1/2}
. Consequently,
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|\geq\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{e}_{i}\|
. The exact same argument as in the proof of
Lemma

8.14
in
Section

C.7
goes through to demonstrate that each term in
{\sf ErrCIPCA}
is
o\big(\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)}\big)
except we must also demonstrate that

\displaystyle\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}=o(1),

(58)

as we do not assume this explicitly.

We therefore verify (
58
). First, we note that

\displaystyle\max_{k\neq j}\bigg(\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\bigg)^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{(s_{\bm{e}_{i},j}^{{\sf PCA}})^{2}}\bigg(\frac{r(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{2}}{n\Delta_{\min}^{2}}\max_{k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}+\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{n\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{2}}{n\Delta_{\min}^{2}}\frac{n\Delta_{j}^{2}}{(\lambda_{\min}+\sigma^{2})^{2}\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}}\max_{k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}{n\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\frac{n\lambda_{j}^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})\sigma^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\kappa^{2}\Delta_{j}^{2}}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}\frac{\max_{k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}}{\min_{k\neq j,k\leq r}|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|^{2}}+\kappa^{3}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r\kappa^{2}\Delta_{j}^{2}}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\kappa^{3},

where we have used the assumption that
|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\asymp|\bm{e}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{l}|
for all
k,l\leq r
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\max_{k\neq j}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{r}\kappa\Delta_{j}}{\Delta_{\min}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad+\kappa^{3/2}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{j}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})r\kappa^{3/2}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{\min}\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle=o(1)

since we assume that
\Delta_{\min}\gtrsim(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\kappa^{3/2}\frac{r^{5/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\log^{4}(n\vee p)
, which completes the proof.
∎

Appendix D
Proofs of Additional Lemmas from Appendix
B

This section contains all of the intermediate lemmas required in
Appendix

B
.

D.1
Proof of Lemma
B.2

Proof.

Note that
\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})
is a submatrix of the matrix

\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}-\gamma(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bm{I}_{r}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}

\displaystyle\bigg(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}-\gamma(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bm{I}_{r}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\lambda:|\lambda|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]}\|\bm{G}(\lambda)-\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\lambda)\|,

where
\bm{G}(\lambda)
and
\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\lambda)
are defined in (
17
) and (
18
) respectively, where we used the fact that with probability
\exp(-cn)
it holds that
|\widehat{\lambda}_{j}|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]
by
4
. By
Lemma

B.1
it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda:|\lambda|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]}\|\bm{G}(\lambda)-\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\lambda)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{rn\log(n)},

which holds with probability
1-O(n^{-10})
. This completes the proof.
∎

D.2
Proof of Lemma
B.3

Proof.

Note that the matrix

\displaystyle(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}

is a submatrix of

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}-\gamma(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bm{I}_{r}=\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\bm{G}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}),

where
\bm{G}(\lambda)
and
\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\lambda)
are defined in (
17
) and (
18
) respectively. Consequently,

\displaystyle\big\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\big\|\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\bm{G}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\lambda:|\lambda|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]}\|\bm{G}(\lambda)-\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\lambda)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{rn\log(n)},

where we used
Lemma

B.1
as well as
4
, which holds on the event
\|\bm{N}\|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{n}
.
∎

D.3
Proof of Lemma
B.4

Proof.

First we will devise a concentration inequality for
\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}
for a fixed deterministic vector
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
, and then complete the proof via
\varepsilon
-net.

Step 1: Concentration for a fixed vector
. Let
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
be any deterministic unit vector. Note that

\displaystyle\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}\bm{N}_{ii^{\prime}}\big(\bm{U}\bm{x}\big)_{i}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}(\bm{u}_{k})_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)

\displaystyle=:\sum_{i,i^{\prime}}\bm{N}_{ii^{\prime}}\bm{c}_{i}\bm{\widetilde{c}}_{i^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{i\leq i^{\prime}}\bm{N}_{ii^{\prime}}\big(\bm{c}_{i}\bm{\widetilde{c}}_{i^{\prime}}+\bm{c}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{\widetilde{c}}_{i}\big),

where we set
\bm{c}_{i}:=\big(\bm{U}\bm{x}\big)_{i}
and
\bm{\widetilde{c}}_{i^{\prime}}:=\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}(\bm{u}_{k})_{i^{\prime}}\bigg)
. By Hoeffding’s inequality, with probability at least
1-c\exp(-ct^{2})
that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{i\leq i^{\prime}}\bm{N}_{ii^{\prime}}\big(\bm{c}_{i}\bm{\widetilde{c}}_{i^{\prime}}+\bm{c}_{i^{\prime}}\bm{\widetilde{c}}_{i}\big)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma t\|\bm{c}\|\|\bm{\widetilde{c}}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma t\|\bm{U}\bm{x}\|\bigg(\sum_{i^{\prime}}\big(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}(\bm{u}_{k})_{i^{\prime}}\big)^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma t\sqrt{r}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}.

Therefore, for any deterministic unit vector
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|\lesssim\sigma t\sqrt{r}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}.

with probability at least
1-c\exp(-ct^{2})
.

Step 2: Union bound
. By taking an
1/4
-net of the sphere in
r
dimensions (see
Vershynin 2018
for details on
\varepsilon
-nets), it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma t\sqrt{r}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

with probability at least
c9^{r}\exp(-ct^{2})
. Taking
t\geq C\sqrt{r\log(n)}
demonstrates that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma r\sqrt{\log(n)}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{a})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
as desired.
∎

D.4
Proof of Lemma
B.5

Proof.

The proof of this result is similar to Lemma 1 of
Li et al. 2022
, only with some slight adjustments. We will proceed in steps. First, note that by
3
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}

\displaystyle\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{|\lambda|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|.

We will first deduce a concentration inequality for any fixed value
\lambda
and then take an
\varepsilon
-net.

Step 1: Concentration for fixed
\lambda
.
Define the matrix

\displaystyle\bm{H}(\lambda):=\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}.

Let
\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times(n-r)}
denote a random matrix with independent
\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
entries, and let
\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\in\mathbb{R}^{(n-r)\times(n-r)}
be defined similarly, independently from
\bm{N}_{\|\perp}
, except with diagonal elements having variance
2\sigma^{2}
. Then

\displaystyle(\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp},\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp})\overset{{{\sf d}}}{=}(\bm{N}_{\|\perp},\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}).

Consequently,
\bm{H}(\lambda)
has the same distribution as

\displaystyle\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}.

We will use an
\varepsilon
-net argument for the
r
-dimensional sphere. Let
\bm{x}
denote a deterministic
r
-dimensional unit vector. Then by Hoeffding’s inequality, with probability at least
1-c\exp(-ct^{2}),

\displaystyle\bigg|\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\sum_{i=1}^{r}\sum_{i^{\prime}=1}^{n-r}\bm{x}_{i}\big(\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big)_{ii^{\prime}}\bigg(\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg)_{i^{\prime}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma t\|\bm{x}\|\bigg\|\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma t\bigg\|\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|.

By taking a
1/4
-net of the sphere in
r
dimensions, we obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma t\bigg\|\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

with probability at least
1-c9^{r}\exp(-ct^{2})
. Take
t=C\sqrt{r\log(n)}
to yield

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}\bigg\|\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
. Consequently, for any
\lambda
satisfying
2|\lambda_{j}|/3\leq|\lambda|\leq 4|\lambda_{j}|/3
we obtain, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-11})
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|,

where we used the fact that by
1
it holds that the eigenvalues of
\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}

are at least
|\lambda|-\|\bm{N}\|\geq 2|\lambda_{j}|/3-\|\bm{N}\|\geq\lambda_{\min}/3
together with the Gaussian concentration inequality
\|\bm{N}\|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{n}
.

Step 2: Completing the argument
. Let
\varepsilon=c|\lambda_{j}|/n
, and let
\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
denote the
\varepsilon
-net for the region
[-4|\lambda_{j}|/3,-2|\lambda_{j}|/3]\cup[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]
. Then
|\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}|\lesssim|\lambda_{j}|/\varepsilon\asymp n
. Therefore, by the union bound, for any
\lambda\in\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\big\|\bm{H}(\lambda)\big\|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}\frac{\sigma\sqrt{n}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

Let
\lambda^{\prime}
be any fixed value, and let
\lambda\in\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
satisfy
|\lambda-\lambda^{\prime}|\leq\varepsilon
. Consequently, conditional on
\|\bm{N}\|\leq\lambda_{\min}/3
, letting
\eta_{i}
denote the eigenvalues of
\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{H}(\lambda)-\bm{H}(\lambda^{\prime})\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}-\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\big(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{N}_{\|\perp}\|\|\bm{N}_{\perp\perp}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\max_{i}\bigg|\frac{1}{\lambda-\eta_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda^{\prime}-\eta_{i}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}n\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\max_{i}\frac{|\lambda-\lambda^{\prime}|}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|,

where the final inequality uses the fact that
\varepsilon=c|\lambda_{j}|/n
. Therefore, define

\displaystyle\lambda^{*}:=\argsup_{|\lambda|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|,

which is attained due to the fact that the set is compact and the fact that the function in question is continuous as a function of
\lambda
(on the set in question, conditional on
\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|\leq\lambda_{\min}/3
). Let
\lambda^{*^{\prime}}
be such that
|\lambda^{*}-\lambda^{*^{\prime}}|\leq\varepsilon
. Then

\displaystyle\sup_{|\lambda|\in[2|\lambda_{j}|/3,4|\lambda_{j}|/3]}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\lambda\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle=\big\|\bm{H}(\lambda^{*})\big\|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\lambda\in\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}}\big\|\bm{H}(\lambda)\big\|+\big\|\bm{H}(\lambda^{*})-\bm{H}(\lambda^{*^{\prime}})\big\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|,

which holds with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
, which completes the proof.
∎

D.5
Proof of Lemma
B.6

Proof.

Let
\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})
be as in
Lemma

8.2
, and let
k\neq j,k\leq r
be fixed. By the eigenvalue-eigenvector equation it holds that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}))

\displaystyle=\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\frac{\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}+\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\frac{\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bigg(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}\widehat{(}\lambda_{j})\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k},

where in the second line we used the fact that
\bm{I}=\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}+\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}
, and in the final line we have implicitly invoked
Theorem

8.1
on the event in
1
, where invertibility of
\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}
is guaranteed by
4
. We have also implicitly assumed that the deminator is nonzero; however, by
Lemma

8.2
with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle|\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})|

\displaystyle\geq|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|-|\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-\lambda_{j}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})|

\displaystyle\geq|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|-\delta^{{\sf MD}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|,

since
\delta^{{\sf MD}}\lesssim\Delta_{j}\leq|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|
, with
\delta^{{\sf MD}}
defined in
Lemma

8.2
. Consequently, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-9})
it holds that

\displaystyle|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}\bigg\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}\widehat{(}\lambda_{j})\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}\bigg\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\|.

By
Lemma

B.2
and
Lemma

B.3
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf MD}}\widehat{(}\lambda_{j})\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}};

\displaystyle\bigg\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{n-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}

with probability
1-O(n^{-10})
. In addition, note that
\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{k}
is a submatrix of
\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}
, and by
2
it holds that
\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}\|\lesssim\delta^{{\sf MD}}
with this same probability. Therefore, combining bounds we arrive at

\displaystyle|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|}\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}}+\delta^{{\sf MD}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\delta^{{\sf MD}}}{|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|},

where the final inequality follows from the assumption that
\lambda_{\min}\geq C_{0}\sigma\sqrt{nr}
for some sufficiently large constant
C_{0}
. The result is completed with a union bound over all
k\neq j
.
∎

D.6
Proof of Lemma
B.7

Proof.

The proof follows along the exact same arguments as the proof of
Lemma

B.5
, only replacing
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}
with
\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}
.
∎

D.7
Proof of Lemma
B.8

Proof.

Recall that
\mathcal{P}_{{\sf Upper-diag}}
denotes the projection onto the upper diagonal of a matrix. We start by noting at the outset that

\displaystyle\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{\widehat{S}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf Upper-diag}}\big(\bm{S}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}+\bm{N}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F},

and hence that

\displaystyle\bigg|\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{\widehat{S}}

\displaystyle-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{S}}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}-\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{N}\big)\|_{F}\bigg|

\displaystyle\leq\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}+\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{S}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}+\|\bm{S}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}.

Furthermore, by
1
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\|\bm{N}\|\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{rn}.

In addition,

\displaystyle\|\bm{S}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{S}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|_{F}+\|\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\|(\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top})\bm{U}\Lambda\bm{U}^{\top}\|_{F}

\displaystyle\leq 2\sqrt{r}\lambda_{1}\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sigma\sqrt{rn},

where the final bound follows from the Davis-Kahan Theorem, both holding with probability
e^{-cn}
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{\widehat{S}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{S}}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}-\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{N}\big)\|_{F}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\kappa\sigma\sqrt{rn}.

(59)

Next, it is straightforward to demonstrate that (e.g., Lemma 1 of
Laurent and Massart 2000
)

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{N}\big)\|_{F}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}-\binom{n}{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\binom{n}{2}\log(n)}+\log(n)\lesssim n\sqrt{\log(n)}

with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{N}\big)\|_{F}}{\sqrt{\binom{n}{2}}}-\sigma\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\frac{\sqrt{\log(n)}}{n}.

Hence, combining this bound with (
59
), we obtain

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{\widehat{S}}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{S}}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|_{F}}{\sqrt{\binom{n}{2}}}-\sigma\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\frac{\|\mathcal{P}_{{\sf upper-diag}}\big(\bm{N})\big)\|_{F}}{\sqrt{\binom{n}{2}}}-\sigma\bigg|+\frac{1}{n}\kappa\sigma\sqrt{rn}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\frac{\sqrt{\log(n)}}{n}+\sigma\frac{\kappa\sqrt{r}}{\sqrt{n}}\lesssim\sigma\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}},

and hence with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\sigma}}{\sigma}-1\bigg|\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}.

Furthermore, this also implies that
\big|\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}-1\big|\lesssim\kappa\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}
with this same probability.
∎

D.8
Proof of Lemma
B.9

Proof.

By
Lemma

8.4
, with probability at least
1-O(n^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}+O\bigg(\kappa\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{rn}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg).

Therefore by
Lemma

8.1
, with this same probability we also have that

\displaystyle\bigg|\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{k}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{n\log(n)}}{\lambda_{k}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{nr}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{nr\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}.

We will now study the difference
\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}
. By the analysis leading to the proof of
Theorem

3.1
, with
j=k
, we have that

\displaystyle\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\bigg(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\bigg)\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}-1\bigg)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}\bigg(\sum_{l\neq k}\frac{\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{l}}{\lambda_{k}-\lambda_{l}}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a}+{\sf ErrMD}\times s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}\bigg)+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg(\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}-1\bigg).

Consequently,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\sum_{l\neq k}\frac{\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{N}\bm{u}_{l}}{\lambda_{k}-\lambda_{l}}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|+{\sf ErrMD}\times s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}-\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf MD}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+{\sf ErrMD}\times s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+{\sf ErrMD}\times s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}.

Therefore, as long as

\displaystyle s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}=o(1),

(60)

it holds that
|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}|\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg|(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}

\displaystyle(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\bigg|\bigg(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}+\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg)\bigg(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf MD}}}}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg(s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}\frac{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}}{s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},j}}\sqrt{\log(n)}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}r\log(n)}{\Delta_{\min}^{2}}+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\frac{\sigma^{2}\kappa\sqrt{rn\log(n)}}{\lambda_{\min}^{2}}\bigg),

provided that (
60
) holds. We verify this now.

Note that it is sufficient to have that

\displaystyle s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}\sqrt{\log(n)}=o(1).

Hence,

\displaystyle s^{{\sf MD}}_{\bm{a},k}\leq\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n)}}{\Delta_{\min}}+\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{j}}=o(1).

This completes the proof.
∎

Appendix E
Proofs of Additional Lemmas from Appendix
C

This section contains all the proofs of the additional lemmas in
Appendix

C
.

E.1
Proof of Lemma
C.2

Proof.

First we note that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma})\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{r}\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma})\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|_{\infty}.

Therefore, we will bound each of the
r
entries and take a union bound.

Fix an index
l
. Note that
\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}=\frac{1}{n}\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}
. Therefore,

\bm{e}_{l}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}=\frac{1}{n}(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}.

As consequence,

\displaystyle\bm{e}_{l}^{\top}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma})\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\bm{e}_{l}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\big(\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}-\bm{I}_{p}\big)\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\big(\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}-\mathbb{I}_{l=k}\big)\frac{(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}.

Observe that the expression above is a sum of independent mean-zero sub-exponential random variables. Let

\displaystyle\bm{S}_{i}

\displaystyle=\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\big(\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\big)\frac{(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\bm{\bm{a}}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}.

To apply Bernstein’s inequality, we need to study the
\psi_{1}
norm of each
\bm{S}_{i}
, which by independence are all the same. We split this into two cases based on whether or not
k=l
.

•

Case 1:
k\neq l
. For a given
l
we note
\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}
is independent from
\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}
by rotational invariance of Gaussian random variables, and moreover,
\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}
and
\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}
are standard Gaussian random variables. Consequently,

\displaystyle\|\bm{S}_{i}\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq,k\leq r}\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\leq\|\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\|_{\psi_{2}}\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\neq l,k\leq r}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\neq l,k\leq r}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg\|_{\psi_{2}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\neq l,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{l}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}
