\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}},

where the penultimate line uses the standard property for sums of independent subgaussian random variables (Proposition 2.6.1 of
Vershynin 2018
).

•

Case 2:
k=l
.
Observe that the summation is a sum of independent sub-exponential random variables. Furthermore, it is straightforward to observe that
\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}-1\|_{\psi_{1}}\lesssim 1
. Therefore,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}

\displaystyle\bigg(\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}-1\bigg)\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg\|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{Y}_{i}\bm{Y}_{i}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}-1\bigg\|_{\psi_{1}}\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\bigg(\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\bigg|\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bigg|^{2}\bigg)^{1/2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}},

where the penultimate inequality is due to Cauchy-Schwarz.

Combining these inequalities, we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{S}_{i}\|_{\psi_{1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}=:K.

Therefore, by Bernstein’s inequality, it holds that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}\bigg\{\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{S}_{i}\bigg|\geq tK\bigg\}

\displaystyle\leq 2\exp\bigg(-cn\min\{t^{2},t\}\bigg).

Therefore, with
t=C\frac{\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}{\sqrt{n}}
, together with the assumption
\log(p)\lesssim n
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\bm{S}_{i}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{r}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}.

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-11})
. As a consequence, taking a union bound over at most
(n\vee p)
entries we obtain

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma})\bm{u}_{k}}{\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}}\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{r(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}{\sqrt{n}}\sqrt{\sum_{k\neq j,k\leq r}\frac{(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}}{(\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k})^{2}}}

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. This completes the proof.
∎

E.2
Proof of Lemma
C.3

Proof.

This holds directly from
Lemma

C.1
since the quantity in question is a submatrix of
\bm{\widetilde{G}}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)-\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)
.
∎

E.3

This holds directly from
Lemma

C.1
since the quantity in question is a submatrix of
\bm{\widetilde{G}}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)-\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)
.
∎

E.4
Proof of Lemma
C.5

Proof.

The proof is by
\varepsilon
-net. Let
\bm{x}\in\mathbb{R}^{r}
be a fixed unit vector. Note that

\displaystyle\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\frac{\sigma}{n}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}.

Note that by rotational invariance
\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}
and
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}
are independent and hence equal in distribution to
\bm{W}\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times n}
and
\bm{Z}\in\mathbb{R}^{p-r\times n}
, where
\bm{W}
and
\bm{Z}
have independent standard Gaussian entries. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}^{1/2}\bm{W}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\|\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}\bm{x}\|^{2}\bm{I}_{n});\qquad\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{Z}\sim\mathcal{N}(0,\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|^{2}\bm{I}_{n}).

Therefore, conditional on
\bm{Z}
, Hoeffding’s inequality implies that

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\sigma}{n}\bm{x}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}^{1/2}\bm{W}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}}{n}\|\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\|\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}^{1/2}\bm{x}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{n}\|\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\ \bm{a}\|\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}

with probability at least
1-\exp(-cr\log(n\vee p))
. Therefore, by unfixing
\bm{x}
, a standard
\varepsilon
-net argument implies that conditional on
\bm{Z}
it holds that

\displaystyle\frac{\sigma}{n}\|\bm{\widetilde{\Lambda}}^{1/2}\bm{W}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{n}\sqrt{r\log(n\vee p)}\|\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

The result is completed as
\|\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\lesssim\sqrt{n\log(n\vee p)}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|
with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
.
∎

E.5
Proof of Lemma
C.6

Proof.

First we note that

\displaystyle\bigg\|

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{r}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sqrt{r}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\sqrt{n}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sup_{\lambda\in[2(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3,4(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3]}\bigg\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|_{\infty},

(61)

where we have impicitly applied
6
.
Therefore, it suffices to bound the quantity above which we will achieve by bounding each entry and then taking a union bound over all entries. To bound each entry, similar to the proof of
Lemma

B.5
in
Section

D.4
we will first derive a concentration inequality for fixed
\lambda
and then unfix
\lambda
.

Step 1: Bounding each entry for fixed
\lambda
. Let
\bm{W}
be a
r\times n
matrix of i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
random variables, and let
\bm{Z}
be a
p-r\times n
matrix with i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,\sigma^{2})
random variables. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}\bm{W}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a},

where
\overset{d}{=}
denotes equality in distribution. Suppose
\bm{Z}/\sqrt{n}
has SVD
\bm{U}^{(\bm{Z})}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}}(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}
. Then it holds that

\displaystyle\bm{W}^{\top}

\displaystyle\frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\bm{Z})}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}}(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg)^{-1}(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle=\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\bm{Z})}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}

\displaystyle\overset{d}{=}\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a},

where we have used the fact that
\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}
,
\bm{\bm{U}}^{(\bm{Z})}
are independent and, in addition,
\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{U}^{(\bm{Z})}\overset{d}{=}\bm{W}
by rotational invariance. Consequently, the terms
\bm{W},\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}
, and
\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}
are mutually independent.

Observe that the
l
’th entry can be written via

\displaystyle\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{n}\bm{W}_{l^{\prime}l}\Bigg[

\displaystyle\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg]_{l^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l^{\prime}=1}^{\min(n,p)}\bm{W}_{l^{\prime}l}\frac{(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}})^{1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}-\sigma^{2})}{\lambda-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}}\bigg(\big(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\big)^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg)_{l^{\prime}}

where we have used the fact that
\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}
is diagonal. Let
\bm{w}
denote the vector with entries given by

\displaystyle\bm{w}_{l^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\bm{W}_{l^{\prime}l}\frac{(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}})^{1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}-\sigma^{2})}{\lambda-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}}.

Then we can write the above quantity via

\displaystyle\langle\bm{w},\big(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\big)^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\rangle.

Conditional on
\bm{W}
and
\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}
, the random variable
\frac{\big(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\big)^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}}{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}
is uniformly distributed on
p-r
-dimensional sphere. Therefore, by standard concentration inequalities for uniform spherical random variables (Theorem 3.4.6 of
Vershynin 2018
) it holds that

\displaystyle\big|\langle\bm{w},\big(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\big)^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{p-r}}\|\bm{w}\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{p}}\|\bm{w}\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

(62)

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-12})
. In addition, conditional on
\bm{Z}
,
\bm{w}
is a Gaussian random vector with diagonal covariance
\bm{C}
given by

\displaystyle\bm{C}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}

\displaystyle=\frac{(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}})^{1/2}(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}-\sigma^{2})}{\lambda-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}}.

Consequently, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-12})
,

\displaystyle\|\bm{w}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{C}\|_{F}\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}\lesssim\sqrt{\min(p-r,n)\log(n\vee p)}\|\bm{C}\|,

(63)

where we have used the fact that
\bm{C}
has only
\min(p-r,n)
nonzero eigenvalues. Since
\bm{C}
is a function of the matrix
\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}
, it therefore suffices to study the singular of the matrix
\bm{Z}/\sqrt{n}
.

First we note that standard concentration for Gaussian covariance matrices imply that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\bm{ZZ}^{\top}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg);

(64)

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{1}{n}\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}-\sigma^{2}\frac{p-r}{n}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\big(1+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\big).

Consequently, since the eigenvalues of
\bm{ZZ}^{\top}
and
\bm{Z}^{\top}\bm{Z}
are the same it holds that

\displaystyle\max_{l^{\prime}}\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\frac{\max(n,p-r)}{n}+\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}},

(65)

which implies that

\displaystyle\max_{l^{\prime}}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}_{l^{\prime}l^{\prime}}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{\max(n,p-r)}{n}}+\bigg(\frac{p}{n}\bigg)^{1/4}\bigg).

Combining this bound with the bound (
64
) and
6
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\|\bm{C}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\max_{l^{\prime}}\frac{\sigma^{3}}{\lambda-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\max(n,p-r)}{n}}+\left(\frac{p}{n}\right)^{1/4}\right)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}}{\lambda_{j}}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\max(n,p)}{n}}+\left(\frac{p}{n}\right)^{1/4}\right)\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\asymp\frac{\sigma^{3}}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}+\frac{p^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}\bigg).

Therefore, plugging in this bound in (
63
) and combining with (
62
) we obtain that

\displaystyle\big|\langle\bm{w},\big(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\big)^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\rangle\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\log(n\vee p)\sqrt{\min(p,n)}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{p}}\bigg(\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{p^{3/2}}{n^{3/2}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\log(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|.

These bounds hold cumulatively with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-11})
. Concequently, we obtain that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{W}^{\top}\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg(\lambda

\displaystyle\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\log(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-12})
.

Step 2: Bounding each entry by unfixing
\lambda
.
We now complete the proof for arbitrary
\lambda
. Let
\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
be an
\varepsilon
-net for the space
[2(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3,4(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3]
, with
\varepsilon=c\lambda_{j}/(n\vee p)
. Let
\lambda^{\prime}\in\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
be such that
|\lambda-\lambda^{\prime}|\leq\varepsilon
. Denote

\displaystyle\bm{F}(\lambda):

\displaystyle=\bm{W}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}.

By a similar argument as previously, letting
\frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}=\bm{U}^{(\bm{Z})}\sqrt{\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}}(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})})^{\top}
, observe that

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\bm{F}(\lambda)-\bm{F}(\lambda^{\prime})\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{W}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\left(\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}-\bigg(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\right)\bigg(\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg)\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\bm{W}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\left(\left(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\right)^{-1}-\left(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\right)^{-1}\right)\left(\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\right)\big(\bm{V}^{(\bm{Z})}\big)^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\bm{W}^{\top}\ \frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\|_{\infty}\bigg\|\left(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\right)^{-1}-\left(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\right)^{-1}\bigg\|\left\|\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p-r}\right\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\bigg\|\bm{W}^{\top}\ \frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\|_{\infty}\bigg\|\left(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\right)^{-1}-\left(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{\Gamma}^{(\bm{Z})}\right)^{-1}\bigg\|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|,

where in the final line have made use of the previous covariance concentration inequality. In addition, we note that standard Gaussian concentration inequalities imply

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{W}^{\top}\ \frac{\bm{Z}}{\sqrt{n}}\bigg\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\|\bm{Z}\|_{F}\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)\min(p-r,n)}{n}}\|\bm{Z}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)\min(p-r,n)}{n}}\bigg(\sqrt{p}+\sqrt{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)\min(p,n)}\bigg(1+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\asymp\sigma\sqrt{(n\vee p)\log(n\vee p)}

which holds by standard bounds on Gaussian random matrices (i.e.
\|\bm{Z}\|\lesssim\sigma(\sqrt{n}+\sqrt{p})
). Consequently,

\displaystyle\|

\displaystyle\bm{F}(\lambda)-\bm{F}(\lambda^{\prime})\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\sigma^{3}\sqrt{(n\vee p)\log(n\vee p)}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg\|\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}-\bigg(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\Bigg\|.

Let
\eta_{i}
denote the eigenvalues of
\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}
. Turning to the remaining term it holds that

\displaystyle\Bigg\|\bigg(\lambda\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}-\bigg(\lambda^{\prime}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{ZZ}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)^{-1}\Bigg\|

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq i\leq p-r}\bigg|\frac{1}{\lambda-\eta_{i}}-\frac{1}{\lambda^{\prime}-\eta_{i}}\bigg|

\displaystyle=\max_{1\leq i\leq p-r}\bigg|\frac{\lambda^{\prime}-\lambda}{(\lambda-\eta_{i})(\lambda^{\prime}-\eta_{i})}\bigg|\lesssim\frac{\varepsilon}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\asymp\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(n\vee p)}.

Therefore, for any
\lambda
and
\lambda^{\prime}
satisfying
|\lambda-\lambda^{\prime}|\leq\varepsilon
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{F}(\lambda)-\bm{F}(\lambda^{\prime})\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\sigma^{3}\sqrt{(n\vee p)\log(n\vee p)}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\frac{1}{\lambda_{j}(n\vee p)}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|\sigma^{3}\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n\vee p}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg).

This bound holds independent of
\lambda
and
\lambda^{\prime}
(provided they are less than
\varepsilon
) with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-12})
. Therefore, let
\lambda^{*}
denote the maximizer, and pick
\lambda^{*^{\prime}}\in\mathcal{E}_{\varepsilon}
satisfying
|\lambda^{*}-\lambda^{*^{\prime}}|\leq\varepsilon
. Then taking a union bound over at most
(n\vee p)
many terms, we arrive at

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\in[2\lambda_{j}/3,4\lambda_{j}/3]}\|\bm{F}(\lambda)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle=\|\bm{F}(\lambda^{*})\|_{\infty}\leq\|\bm{F}(\lambda^{*^{\prime}})\|_{\infty}+\|\bm{F}(\lambda^{*^{\prime}})-\bm{F}(\lambda)\|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\log(n\vee p)\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{3}\sqrt{\log(n\vee p)}\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n\vee p}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{3}\log(n\vee p)\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}\|}{\lambda_{j}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg).

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. Therefore, combining this bound with (
61
) we achieve the desired result.
∎

E.6
Proof of Lemma
C.7

Proof.

We slightly modify the proof of
Lemma

B.6
. We note that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\big)

\displaystyle=\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}\Bigg(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\Bigg)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}\Bigg(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}\Bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})}\Bigg(\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\Bigg),

where we have implicitly assumed that the denominator is nonzero. However, by
Lemma

8.9
, we note that

\displaystyle\bigg|\widehat{\lambda}_{j}-(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\big(1+\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\bigg|

\displaystyle=(1+\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}))\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{j}}{1+\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})}-\lambda_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle=(1+o(1))|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|-\delta^{{\sf PCA}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim|\lambda_{j}-\lambda_{k}|-\delta^{{\sf PCA}}

\displaystyle\gtrsim\Delta_{j},

as long as
\Delta_{j}\gg\delta^{{\sf PCA}}
, which is guaranteed by the noise assumption (
4
). Consequently,

\displaystyle|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|+\frac{1}{\Delta_{j}}\bigg|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|.

By a similar argument to the proof of
Lemma

8.7
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}.

In addition, by
Theorem

8.2
and
7
we have that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}

\displaystyle=\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}

Consequently,

\displaystyle\bigg|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}

\displaystyle\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\bigg)\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{U}^{-k}(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg|\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}-\gamma^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg\|(\bm{U}^{-k})^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big(\widehat{\lambda}_{j}\bm{I}_{p-r}-\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)^{-1}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\bm{u}_{k}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})-\bm{\widetilde{G}}^{{\sf PCA}}(\widehat{\lambda}_{j})\|

where
\bm{G}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)
and
\bm{\widetilde{G}}^{{\sf PCA}}(\lambda)
are defined in
Section

C.2
. By
Lemma

C.1
with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\sup_{\lambda\in[2(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3,4(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})/3]}\|\bm{G}(\lambda)-\bm{\widetilde{G}}(\lambda)\|

\displaystyle\lesssim(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{j}^{2}}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)\sqrt{\frac{r}{n}}\log^{2}(n\vee p)

\displaystyle\ll\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}.

As a consequence,

\displaystyle|\bm{\widehat{u}}_{j}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\Delta_{j}}.

Therefore, the result is completed by taking a union bound over all
k\neq j
with
k\leq r
.
∎

E.7
Proof of Lemma
C.8

Proof.

The proof follows
mutatis mutandis
the proof of
Lemma

C.6
only with the replacement
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}
instead of
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{a}.

∎

E.8
Proof of Lemma
C.9

Proof.

First suppose that
p>n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)
. Recall that

\displaystyle\bm{X}=\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bm{Y}=\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{Y}+\sigma\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y},

where
\bm{Y}
consists of independent standard Gaussian random variables.
Suppose that

\displaystyle\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}

\displaystyle=\bm{\widetilde{U}}\bm{\Gamma}\bm{\widetilde{U}}^{\top},

where it is readily seen that
\bm{\Gamma}
has at most
r
nonzero eigenvalues.
Define the event

\displaystyle\mathcal{A}

\displaystyle:=\bigg\{\lambda_{\min}\lesssim\bm{\Gamma}_{ii}\lesssim\lambda_{\max}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\bigg\|\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}-\bm{I}_{p}\bigg\|\lesssim\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg\}

\displaystyle\qquad\bigcap\bigg\{\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}-\bm{I}_{p}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\|\lesssim\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg\}.

By a similar analysis to
Lemma

8.7
it holds that

\displaystyle\|\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\bigg(\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}-\bm{I}_{p}\bigg)\bm{U}\bm{\Lambda}^{1/2}\bm{U}^{\top}\|\lesssim\lambda_{\max}\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\lesssim\kappa\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}},

which implies that
\bm{\Gamma}_{ii}\gtrsim\lambda_{\min}
. Similarly,
\max_{i}\bm{\Gamma}_{ii}\lesssim\lambda_{\max}
. Therefore, it is readily seen that
\mathbb{P}(\mathcal{A})\geq 1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
by the union bound.

We have that

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}=\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}+\sigma^{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}+\sigma^{2}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top},

and hence

\displaystyle\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{r}

\displaystyle=\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}+\sigma^{2}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}-\sigma^{2}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{Y}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\sigma^{2}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{Y}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top},

which implies that

\displaystyle\Bigg|{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}

\displaystyle-\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{r}\bigg)-\sigma^{2}{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\underbrace{\bigg\|\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bigg\|}_{\beta_{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r}\sigma^{2}\underbrace{\bigg\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bigg\|}_{\beta_{2}}

\displaystyle\quad+\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r}\underbrace{\bigg\|\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{Y}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bigg\|}_{\beta_{3}},

where we have used the fact that
|{\sf{Tr}}(\bm{M})|\leq\sqrt{{\sf Rank}(\bm{M})}\|\bm{M}\|
. We analyze each term in turn.

•

The term
\beta_{1}
. For
\beta_{1}
, we have that

\displaystyle\beta_{1}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}\bigg\|+\bigg\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{\widetilde{U}\Gamma\widetilde{U}}^{\top}\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{\widetilde{U}}\bigg\|\lambda_{\max},

where the final bound holds with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
.

Observe that
\bm{\widetilde{U}}
are the eigenvectors of the matrix
\bm{\widetilde{U}}\bm{\Gamma}\bm{\widetilde{U}}^{\top}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}
, which has
r
’th largest eigenvalue at least
\lambda_{\min}+\sigma^{2}
on the event
\mathcal{A}
. In addition, on this event it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg\|\sigma^{2}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg).

Therefore, viewing
\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}
as a perturbation of
\bm{\widetilde{U}}\bm{\Gamma}\bm{\widetilde{U}}^{\top}+\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}
, we have that

\displaystyle\|\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\widetilde{U}}\bm{\Gamma}\bm{\widetilde{U}}^{\top}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}\|\leq\bigg\|\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}-\sigma^{2}\bm{I}_{p}\bigg\|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg).

Consequently, by Weyl’s inequality,

\displaystyle\bigg|\lambda_{r+1}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}\big)-\sigma^{2}\bigg|\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

which implies that

\displaystyle\lambda_{r+1}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}})\lesssim\sigma^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)\ll\lambda_{\min}+\sigma^{2},

which holds by the noise assumption (
4
). Therefore,

\displaystyle\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{\widetilde{U}}\|\lesssim\frac{\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)}{\lambda_{\min}}.

Hence, on this event it holds that

\displaystyle\beta_{1}\lesssim\kappa\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg).

This bound is deterministic on the event
\mathcal{A}
, which holds with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
.

•

The term
\beta_{2}
. For
\beta_{2}
, we observe that

\displaystyle\beta_{2}

\displaystyle=\bigg\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\|^{2}\bigg\|\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg),

where the final line is due to
5
, the Davis-Kahan Theorem, and the noise assumption
4
.

•

The term
\beta_{3}
. Note that

\displaystyle\beta_{3}

\displaystyle\leq\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\|+\|\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\bm{U}^{\top}\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\|

\displaystyle\leq 2\|\big(\bm{I}-\bm{\widehat{U}}\bm{\widehat{U}}^{\top}\big)\bm{U}\|\|\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\|\bm{U}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg(1+\sqrt{\frac{r\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}

where the penultimate inequality follows from the covariance concentration for Gaussian random variables, and the final inequality is due to the assumption on
r
.

Combining our bounds for
\beta_{1}
,
\beta_{2}
, and
\beta_{3}
, we obtain that

\displaystyle\Bigg|{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{r}\bigg)-\sigma^{2}{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg)\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r}\sigma^{2}\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg(1+\frac{p}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r}\sigma^{2}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}},

which holds with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
. As a result,

\displaystyle\Bigg|\frac{{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}_{r}\bigg)}{\sigma^{2}p-r}-\frac{1}{p-r}{\sf{Tr}}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bigg)\Bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{1}{pn}}+\frac{1}{p}\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r}\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{1}{pn}}+\frac{1}{p}\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\sqrt{r}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}p}.

Furthermore, by Lemma 1 of
Laurent and Massart 2000
with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\|\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\|_{F}^{2}-\sigma^{2}(p-r)n\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{(p-r)n\log(n\vee p)}+\log(n\vee p)\lesssim\sigma^{2}\sqrt{pn\log(n\vee p)}.

Consequently,

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{{\sf{Tr}}\big(\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}\big)}{\sigma^{2}n(p-r)}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}.

Therefore, combining our bounds, we obtain

\displaystyle\bigg|\frac{\widehat{\sigma}^{2}}{\sigma^{2}}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}+\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{1}{pn}}+\frac{1}{p}\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\qquad+\sqrt{r}\bigg(\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg)^{2}\bigg(\frac{1}{p}+\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{1}{pn}}+\frac{1}{p}\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{n}}\bigg)+\sqrt{r}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{r}\kappa\frac{\log^{4}(n\vee p)}{n}+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}},

with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
, where we have used the assumption that
p>n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)
. This proves the first assertion.

We now prove the second assertion, where we assume that
p\leq n/\log^{4}(n\vee p)
, which in particular implies
p\leq n
. First, by the Poincare Separation Theorem (Corollary 4.3.37 of
Horn and Johnson 2012
) it holds that

\displaystyle\widehat{\lambda}_{r+1}\leq\lambda_{r}\bigg(\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\frac{\bm{XX}^{\top}}{n}\bm{U}_{\perp}\bigg)=\sigma^{2}\lambda_{r}\bigg(\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{YY}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg).

Note that
\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{Y}
is a
p-r\times n
-dimensional matrix of independent standard Gaussian random variables. Therefore, the covariance concentration inequality (e.g.,
Lemma

8.7
) implies that with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
,

\displaystyle\bigg\|\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{YY}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}-\bm{I}_{p-r}\bigg\|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(p\vee n)}{n}},

(66)

since
p\leq n
. Therefore, Weyl’s inequality implies that

\displaystyle\sigma^{2}\lambda_{r}\bigg(\frac{\bm{U}_{\perp}^{\top}\bm{YY}^{\top}\bm{U}_{\perp}}{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\leq\sigma^{2}+O\bigg(\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(p\vee n)}{n}}\bigg).

Next, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{\lambda}_{r+1}

\displaystyle=\lambda_{r+1}\bigg(\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\frac{\bm{YY}^{\top}}{n}\bm{\Sigma}^{1/2}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma^{2}\lambda_{r+1}\bigg(\frac{\bm{Y}\bm{Y}^{\top}}{n}\bigg)

\displaystyle\geq\sigma^{2}-O\bigg(\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sigma^{2}\sqrt{\frac{\log(p\vee n)}{n}}\bigg),

where we have applied Weyl’s inequality and the bound (
66
). Consequently, combining the upper and lower bound, we have that

\displaystyle\big|\frac{\widehat{\lambda}_{r+1}}{\sigma^{2}}-1\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(p\vee n)}{n}},

which holds with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
, which completes the proof.
∎

E.9
Proof of Lemma
C.10

Proof.

First by
Lemma

8.12
it holds that

\displaystyle\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}=\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}+O\bigg(\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\bigg(\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}\bigg),

for
p>n
, and where the
O(\cdot)
term above is zero for
p\leq n
.
Furthermore, by
Lemma

8.8
, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-10})
it holds that

\displaystyle\bigg|1-\sqrt{1+b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{k}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{k}\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg).

Therefore, if
p>n
,

\displaystyle\bigg|\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}-1\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{k}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}}{\lambda_{k}\sqrt{n}}\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\bigg(\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})(\lambda_{k}+\sigma^{2})r\log(n\vee p)}{\Delta_{k}^{2}n}

\displaystyle\quad+\bigg(\frac{\sigma}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}}{\lambda_{\min}\sqrt{n}}\bigg)\bigg(\frac{\sigma\kappa\sqrt{r}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}^{1/2}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}+\frac{\sigma^{2}\sqrt{r\kappa}\log^{2}(n\vee p)}{\lambda_{\min}}\sqrt{\frac{p}{n}}\bigg)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\sigma^{2}p}{\lambda_{\min}n}\bigg(\sqrt{r}\kappa\bigg(\frac{1}{n}+\sqrt{\frac{\log(n\vee p)}{np}}\bigg)+\frac{\sqrt{r}}{p}\frac{\mathcal{E}^{{\sf PCA}}}{\lambda_{\min}}

\displaystyle=\mathcal{E}_{b},

where the same bound holds with
p\leq n
by observing that the final term is zero.
By the analysis leading to the proof of
Theorem

3.3
, we have that with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-8})
,

\displaystyle\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\big(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}-\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\big)\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg|+\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\big|\big|1-\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\big|

\displaystyle\leq\bigg|\bigg(\sum_{l\neq k}\frac{\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\big(\bm{\widehat{\Sigma}}-\bm{\Sigma}\big)\bm{u}_{l}}{\lambda_{k}-\lambda_{l}}(\bm{u}_{l}^{\top}\bm{a})+s^{{\sf PCA}}_{\bm{a},k}\times{\sf ErrPCA}\bigg)\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\bigg|

\displaystyle\quad+\big|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}\big|\big|1-\bm{u}_{k}\bm{u}_{k}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}\sqrt{1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}}}\big|

\displaystyle\lesssim s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}\bigg(\log(n\vee p)+{\sf ErrPCA}\bigg)+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\mathcal{E}_{b}

\displaystyle\lesssim s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\log(n\vee p)+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\mathcal{E}_{b}.

Observe that under the conditions of
Theorem

4.2
,

\displaystyle s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}\leq\frac{\sqrt{r}(\lambda_{\max}+\sigma^{2})}{\Delta_{\min}\sqrt{n}}+\frac{(\lambda_{j}+\sigma^{2})^{1/2}\sigma}{\lambda_{j}\sqrt{n}}\lesssim\frac{1}{\log(n\vee p)},

and hence it holds that
|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k}|\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|.
Therefore, with probability at least
1-O((n\vee p)^{-8})

\displaystyle\bigg|(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{\widehat{u}}_{k})^{2}(1+\widehat{b_{k}^{{\sf PCA}}})-(\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k})^{2}\bigg|

\displaystyle\lesssim|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\bigg(s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}\frac{s_{\bm{a},k}^{{\sf PCA}}}{s_{\bm{a},j}^{{\sf PCA}}}\log(n\vee p)+|\bm{a}^{\top}\bm{u}_{k}|\mathcal{E}_{b}\bigg)

which completes the proof.
∎
